Let's design algorithm with insights:
1. Use FFT-based exact convolutions for speed. Need compute full conv length 2n-1. We can use scipy.fft or numpy.fft. For n~4000, FFT length L next power of two >=2n-1 maybe 8192. Convolution c = ifft(fft(a,L)*fft(a,L)).real. Since a nonnegative, c nonnegative up to numerical. This is O(L log L) ~8192*13=106k per conv, much faster than np.convolve O(n^2). But FFT in Python/scipy overhead maybe 0.005s? Good. Gradient: p length L? For smooth objective, need g_j=2∑_k p_k a_{k-j}. This is cross-correlation. Can compute via FFT: g = 2 * ifft(fft(p_conj?) * fft(a)). Let's derive.
We have p_k defined for k=0..2n-2 (and zeros to L). Want g_j = 2∑_{k} p_k a_{k-j}, where a_i only 0..n-1. This is (p * a_rev?)? Let define index. For each k, term a_{k-j}; valid if 0≤k-j≤n-1. So g_j =2∑_{i=0}^{n-1} p_{j+i} a_i. This is cross-correlation of p and a: (p ⋆ a)_j =∑_i p_{j+i} a_i. This is valid mode of np.correlate(p, a, 'valid') returns length n. Helpers:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(x):
    if x <=1: return 1
    p=1
    while p < x: p <<=1
    return p

def _fft_len_for_n(n):
    return _next_pow2(2*n-1)

def _norm1(a, n=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if n is None: n = a.size
    if s < 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return a / n
    return a / s
```
But if a length not n? For central build length n. For projection keep length.

```
def _conv(a, L):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    spec = fa * fa
    c = np.fft.irfft(spec, n=L)
    c = c[:2*a.size-1]
    # remove tiny negatives
    if c.size and c.min() < 0: c = np.maximum(c,0)
    return c
```
Since a real but spec positive; tiny imaginary none. Use fa.real? fa*fa = |fa|^2? For complex fa, fa*fa not |fa|^2! Oops: fa is complex; fa*fa = exp(2iθ)|A|² not correct. Need |fa|^2 = fa * np.conj(fa). Important. Use spec = fa * fa.conj(). irfft gives real. For convolution of real a with itself, FFT spectrum |A|². Yes.

```
def _correlate_w_a(w, a, L, fa=None):
    # w length 2n-1 padded L
    # H[j]=sum_i a_i w_{j+i}
    if fa is None: fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,n=L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(W * fa.conj(), n=L)  # Check conj? earlier S*conj(A).
    return h[:a.size]
```
Let's verify with small using mental. a=[a0,a1]. L>=3. w=[w0,w1,w2]. W*conj(A) irfft y_m=∑_i a_i w_{m+i mod L}? Formula earlier: ifft(P*conj(A)) =∑ a_i p_{m+i}. Yes. So H length. Good.
Alternative check m=0: y0=∑ a_i w_i (circular). For linear, if i=0 w0, i=1 w1. H0 desired ∑_{j?} a_i w_{0+i}=a0w0+a1w1. okay. m=1: a0w1+a1w2, but if L enough and w beyond 2n no terms; w_{3}? zero. Good.

For hard H with top weights? We'll use full w.

- Objective:
```
def _obj(a, n, L):
    c=_conv(a,L)
    mc=float(c.max())
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <=0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*mc/(s*s))
```
If a normalized sum1, returns 2n mc.
We might want just max_c to avoid sum if normalized. Use objective with sum for safety.

- Softmax log:
```
def _log_softmax_weights(c, T, floor_log=None):
    # returns s length c
    c_safe = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0)
    # if all zero uniform? won't
    logc = np.log(c_safe) # -inf for zero
    mx = np.max(logc)
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return np.full_like(c,1.0/c.size)
    x = (logc - mx) / T
    # clip to avoid inf? underflow okay
    x = np.maximum(x, -50.0) # maybe -745 underflow; -50 enough
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = e / e.sum()
    # If e sum zero due all -inf? handled
    return s
```
Using -50 gives weights min 1.9e-22, enough. But if T very small, many zero. Good.

- Log gradient:
```
def _log_grad(a, c, L, fa, T):
    n=a.size
    s = _log_softmax_weights(c, T)
    w = np.zeros_like(c)
    pos = c > 1e-300
    w[pos] = s[pos] / c[pos]
    H = _correlate_w_a(w, a, L, fa)
    G = 2.0 * a * H - 2.0 * a
    G -= np.mean(G)
    return G
```
But if c max small ~1e-4, c_safe. T in log units. Good.

- Multiplicative update:
```
def _mul_update(a, G, eta, n, floor):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)): return a
    gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
    if gscale < 1e-15: return a
    expo = -eta * G / gscale
    expo = np.clip(expo, -30.0, 30.0)
    b = a * np.exp(expo)
    b = np.maximum(b, floor)
    s=b.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return a
    return b/s
```
If a has zeros, floor adds. floor maybe 1e-12/n? For n=4000, 2.5e-16; but underflow. Maybe floor=1e-15. However adding floor to all zeros may add mass and distort; okay.

- Projected update (optional): For c-domain gradient H with s=softmax(c/(Tc)). Use Tc in c units. But maybe not needed.

- Line search in log descent:
```
def _log_step(a, G, obj, n, L, eta, max_back=4):
    gscale=maxabs(G)
    if gscale<1e-15: return a,obj,False
    cur_obj=obj
    best_a=a
    e=eta
    for _ in range(max_back):
        cand=_mul_update(a, G, e/n? not)
        c2=_conv(cand,L); mc2=c2.max(); obj2=2*n*mc2 # sum maybe 1 after norm
        if obj2 < cur_obj - 1e-12:
            return cand,obj2,True
        e *= 0.5
    # optional accept if smoothed obj? or no
    return a,obj,False
```
Need G scaled? _mul_update already scales by maxabs, so eta is dimensionless. Good.

But computing _conv(cand) each backtrack. We can compute c2 and maybe also c candidates? okay.

- Smooth descent run:
```
def _log_descent(a0,n,budget_s, T_start, T_end, eta0, tag, verbose=True):
    L=_fft_len_for_n(n)
    a=_norm1(a0,n)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=_obj(a,n,L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)

- Block perturb function:
```
def _perturb(a, rng, sigma, width_frac=0.05):
    n=a.size
    w=max(2,int(n*width_frac))
    s=rng.integers(0,n-w+1)
    b=a.copy()
    b[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,size=w))
    # avoid zeros
    b=np.maximum(b,1e-300)
    return b/b.sum()
```
Also global: width=n, sigma small.

Let's write helper `_central_seq(n)`:
```
def _central_seq(n):
    u=np.empty(n); u[0]=1.0
    for i in range(n-1):
       u[i+1]=u[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2) # for m=i from 0: ratio (2m+1)/(2m+2)
    s=u.sum(); return u/s
```

Let's detail `_log_descent` current/best logic:
```
def _log_descent(a0,n,deadline,T_init,eta_init,tag, allow_perturb=True):
    L=_fft_len_for_n(n)
    a=_normalize(a0,n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    c=_conv_from_fft(fa,n,L) # fa*fa.conj
    obj=2*n*float(c.max())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=obj
    T=T_init; eta=eta_init
    last_best_time=time.monotonic()
    stall=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       now=time.monotonic()
       mc=float(c.max()); obj=2*n*mc
       if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
           best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); last_best_time=now; stall=0
       # If no improvement for > 45s, perturb current
       if now - last_best_time > 45.0:
           # choose sigma based on time since improve
           age=now-last_best_time
           sig=0.05 + min(0.3, 0.02*age/30)
           a = _perturb(a, rng?, sig, random width)
           # recompute c
           fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L); c=_conv_from_fft(fa,n,L)
           last_best_time=now  # reset? but track best? Maybe use last_perturb.
           stall=0
           it=0
           T = max(0.1, T*1.5) # broaden after perturb
           continue
       G=_log_grad(...)
       gscale=... 
       if gscale small: break
       cand,obj_cand = _log_step_backtrack(a,G,eta,n,L)
       if obj_cand < obj:
           a=cand; obj=obj_cand; fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); c=_conv(a,L); stall=0
           # adapt eta: if improvement > 1e-5 maybe eta*=1.05; else keep
       else:
           stall += 1
           if stall % 50 ==0:
               T=max(0.01, T*0.8)
               eta=max(0.02, eta*0.7)
           # if stall huge and no perturb soon? perturb threshold covers.
       it+=1
       if it % 2000 ==0: print(...)
    return best_a,best_obj
```

Let's implement top-level functions in detail.

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(x): ...

def _norm1(a): ... (assumes length)

def _proj1(a): ...

def _convolve(a, L, fa=None):
    if fa is None: fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    spec=fa*fa.conj()
    c=np.fft.irfft(spec,n=L)
    m=2*a.size-1
    c=c[:m]
    if c.size:
        mn=c.min()
        if mn < 0:
            c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return fa,c

def _corr_w_a(w,a,L,fa=None):
    m=a.size
    if w.size < L:
       wp=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
       wp[:w.size]=w
       w=wp
    if fa is None: fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,n=L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(W*fa.conj(),n=L)
    h=h[:m]
    return h
```
We must be careful: For correlation, if w length m=2n-1, h formula includes circular terms for j near n? We only take j=0..n-1. Could circular wrap from w_{j+i} >=L? no because L>=2n-1 and max j+i≤n-1+n-1=2n-2 < L, so no wrap. Good.

- `_obj(a, n, L, fa=None, c=None)`:
```
if c is None:
  fa,c=_convolve(a,L,fa)
mc=float(c.max()); s=float(a.sum())
if s<=0: return np.inf
return float(2.0*n*mc/(s*s))
```
For normalized sum1, s=1. If using sum1, objective=2nmc/s².

```

- `_log_grad(a,c,L,fa,T)`:
```

pos=c>1e-300
w[pos]=s[pos]/c[pos]
H=_corr_w_a(w,a,L,fa)
G=2*a*H - 2*a
G -= np.mean(G)
return G
```
If H max huge? For central, w~1/c ~5000, a~0.01, H~? w sum? H finite. gscale maybe ~1? Good.

- `_mul_update(a,G,eta,n=None)`:
```

# For positivity and sum, add floor

s=b.sum()
if s<1e-300 or not finite: return a
return b/s
```

- `_proj_update(a,g,eta)`:

- `_backtrack_smooth_update(a,G,obj,n,T,mode)`: Maybe separate log/c.

def _step_log(a,G,obj,n,T?...):
 e=eta
 for back in range(3):
   cand=_mul_update(a,G,e)
   fa,c=_convolve(cand,L)
   obj2=2*n*c.max()
   if obj2 < obj - 1e-14: return cand,obj2,fa,c,True
   e*=0.5
 return None
```

- `_perturb(a,rng,sigma,width_frac)`:

- `_run_log(a0,n,L,T_init,eta_init,run_end,tag,rng,allow_perturb=True)`:
Detailed:
```
def _run_log(...):
    a=_norm1(a0)
    fa,c=_convolve(a,L)
    obj=float(2.0*n*c.max())  # sum 1
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=obj
    T=T_init; eta=eta_init
    last_progress=time.monotonic()
    last_best=time.monotonic()
    last_best_val=best_obj
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < run_end:
       now=time.monotonic()
       mc=float(c.max()); obj=2*n*mc
       if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
           best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=now; 
           # maybe print if significant
       # perturbation logic
       if allow_perturb:
          if now - last_progress > 30.0:
             sig=0.05
             a=_perturb(a,rng,sig, rng.choice([0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,1.0]))
             fa,c=_convolve(a,L)
             last_progress=now
             # widen T after perturb to explore
             T=min(T*1.5, 1.0)
             it=0
             continue
          if now - last_best > 90.0:
             sig=0.15
             a=_perturb(best_a,rng,sig, rng.choice([0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]))
             fa,c=_convolve(a,L)
             last_progress=now
             T=min(T*2.0, 1.0)
             it=0
             continue
       else:
          # just break on stall? 
       G=_log_grad(a,c,L,fa,T)
       gscale=float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
       if gscale < 1e-14:
           # no move, perturb if allowed else break
           if allow_perturb and now-last_progress>5:
              a=_perturb(a,rng,0.05,0.1)
              fa,c=_convolve(a,L); last_progress=now; continue
           break
       improved=False
       e=eta
       for back in range(3):
           cand=_mul_update(a,G,e)
           fa2,c2=_convolve(cand,L)
           obj2=2*n*float(c2.max())
           if obj2 < obj - 1e-14:
              a=cand; obj=obj2; fa=fa2; c=c2; improved=True; break
           e*=0.5
       if improved:
           last_progress=now
           # adapt eta and T
           if obj < best_obj - 1e-9:
               eta = min(eta*1.03, 1.0)
           # slowly anneal T
           T = max(0.02, T*0.9995)
       else:
           # no improve: reduce T/eta
           if (it % 50)==0:
              T = max(0.01, T*0.9)
              eta = max(0.02, eta*0.8)
       it += 1
       if it % 2000 ==0:
           print(f"[{tag}] it={it} T={T:.4f} obj={obj:.6f} best={best_obj:.6f}")
    return best_a,best_obj
```

- `it % 50` only when it increments; if many perturb continues, it reset, okay.
- If no best improvement for 90s, perturb best. But current a may already lost; okay.
- We need `last_progress` updated when accepted even if not best. Yes.
- Starting best maybe parent 1.517. If log descent finds no improvement for 90s, it perturbs best to worse and resets. It may fluctuate. Good.
- Could get stuck in loop where perturbation current makes objective much worse, then gradient takes long to improve, but best safe; after 90s no best, perturb best again. Maybe waste but 2400s enough.

Let's code in mind.

`_norm1`:
```
def _norm1(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a/s
```

`_proj1` similar but if empty? not.

`_convolve`:
```
def _convolve(a, L, fa=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if fa is None:
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    spec=fa*fa.conj()
    c=np.fft.irfft(spec,n=L)
    m=2*a.size-1
    c=c[:m]
    if c.size and c.min()<0.0:
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return fa,c
```
Maybe spec has tiny imaginary? fa*fa.conj real positive but float complex. irfft returns real.

`_corr_w_a`:
```
def _corr_w_a(w,a,L,fa=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if w.size < L:
       wp=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.float64)
       wp[:w.size]=w
       w=wp
    else:
       w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64)[:L]
    if fa is None:
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,n=L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(W*fa.conj(),n=L)
    return h[:n]
```
W real? okay.

`_objective`:
```
def _objective(a,n,L,fa=None,c=None):
    if c is None:
       fa,c=_convolve(a,L,fa)
    mc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s<=0: return float('inf')
    return float(2.0*n*mc/(s*s))
```
Use sum for safety.

`_log_weights`:
```
def _log_weights(c,T):
    n=c.size
    c_safe=np.where(c>1e-300,c,0.0)
    mx=float(np.max(c_safe))
    if mx <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n,1.0/n,dtype=np.float64)
    logc=np.log(c_safe)
    # logc zeros are -inf where safe zero
    mxlog=float(np.max(logc)) # over positive only? if logc has -inf max finite
    if not np.isfinite(mxlog):
       idx=np.argmax(c)
       w=np.zeros(n); w[idx]=1; return w
    x=(logc-mxlog)/T
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, -745.0)
    x=np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(x)
    ss=float(e.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
       idx=np.argmax(c); w=np.zeros(n); w[idx]=1.0; return w
    return e/ss
```
If c huge/small? log safe. T positive.

`_log_gradient`: if c finite; ensure a sum maybe not 1? It uses -2*a/s? The ratio objective derivative with normalized p=a/S: G=2aH*(S?)? If a not sum1, use p=a/s. Our a always normalized but for safety:
```
s=float(a.sum())
if s<=0: zero
p=a/s
H=corr(w,a*L? Note w uses s/c where c conv of a. d=2a_j a_shift/c independent of scale. G=2p? Wait if a unnormalized, log c derivative wrt b: 2a_j a_shift/c. -2logS derivative = -2p. So G=2a*H -2p, where H=∑w a_shift? w=s_lag/c. Yes H dimension a. a*H dimension a²; p dimension a. Dimensions not matching if a not sum1? a*H (a²) vs p (a). But weights w=ν/c, c is a², H=∑ (ν/c) a_shift = ν * a/a²? dimension 1/a. a*H dimension1? Wait a dimension units; c dimension a²; w 1/a; H a*(1/a)=dimensionless; a*H dimension a; p dimension a if S not 1? Actually p=a/S dimensionless if a has units? Objective scale invariant. Let's not worry if normalized sum1: p=a. If not normalized, multiply update? We normalize. In `_log_gradient` assume sum1.

```
def _log_gradient(a,c,L,fa,T):
    s_w=_log_weights(c,T)
    w=np.zeros_like(c)
    pos=c>1e-300
    w[pos]=s_w[pos]/c[pos]
    H=_corr_w_a(w,a,L,fa)
    G=2.0*a*H - 2.0*a
    G-=float(np.mean(G))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
       G=np.zeros_like(a)
    return G
```
If a sum1, okay.

`_c_weights`:
```
def _c_weights(c,T,n):
    r=c*float(n)
    mx=float(np.max(r))
    if mx<=0: uniform
    x=(r-mx)/T
    e=np.exp(np.clip(x,-745,0))
    ss=e.sum()
    if ss<=0: top
    return e/ss
```

`_c_gradient` similar.

`_mul_update`:
```
def _mul_update(a,G,eta):
    gscale=float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
    if gscale < 1e-15: return a.copy()
    expo=np.clip(-eta*G/gscale, -40.0, 40.0)
    # preserve nonfinite
    factor=np.exp(expo)
    b=a*factor
    b=np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    ss=float(b.sum())
    if ss < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ss): return a.copy()
    return b/ss
```
If G mean subtracted, sum factor not 1; normalize.

`_proj_update`:
```
def _proj_update(a,g,eta):
    gs=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if gs<1e-15: return a.copy()
    cand=a - eta*g/gs
    return _proj1(cand)
```

`_central` with recurrence, ensure no overflow. For n up to 12000, u decays, fine. Use loop. Could vectorize with recurrence.

u=_central_raw? But _central normalized. We need u raw maybe not normalized for sym.
```
Define `_central_raw(n)` returns unnormalized u. Then `_central` normalize, `_central_sym` sym raw normalize.
```
def _central_raw(n): ...
def _central(n): u=_central_raw(n); return u/u.sum()
def _central_sym(n): u=_central_raw(n); v=u+u[::-1]; return v/v.sum()
```
Ok.

`_random_seq`:
```
def _random_seq(n,rng,kind=0):
    i=np.arange(n)
    if kind==0:
       x=np.abs(rng.normal(1.0,0.7,n))
       x *= np.exp(-0.3*i/n)
    elif kind==1:
       x=1.0 + 0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.random()*i)
    else:
       x=rng.uniform(0.2,1.5,n)
    x=np.maximum(x,0.05)
    return _norm1(x)
```
No lambdas.

`_ones_norm`.

`_perturb`:
```
def _perturb(a,rng,sigma,width=None):
    n=a.size
    if width is None or width>=1.0:
       w=n; s=0
    else:
       w=max(2,int(n*width))
       if w>n: w=n
       s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
    b=a.copy()
    b[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=w))
    b=np.where(np.isfinite(b),b,0.0)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    ss=b.sum()
    if ss<1e-300: return _ones_norm(n)
    return b/ss
```
Maybe width from rng.choice. In function call, use `float(rng.choice([...]))`; no lambda.

Now `_run_log` and `_run_c` in detail. Need pass L for conv candidate. Use local variables.

`_run_log` parameters: `a0, n, L, T_init, eta_init, run_end, tag, rng`. It can also take `fno_perturb`? Use default True.
Pseudo detailed:
```
def _run_log(a0,n,L,T_init,eta_init,run_end,tag,rng):
    a=_norm1(a0)
    fa,c=_convolve(a,L)
    obj=float(2.0*n*float(c.max()))
    best_a=a.copy(); best_obj=obj
    T=T_init; eta=eta_init
    last_progress=time.monotonic(); last_best=time.monotonic()
    it=0; last_print=0
    while time.monotonic() < run_end:
       now=time.monotonic()
       # objective from c maybe a normalized; update best
       mc=float(c.max()); obj=2.0*n*mc
       if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
          best_obj=obj; best_a=a.copy(); last_best=now
       # perturb logic
       if now - last_progress > 30.0:
          sig=0.04 + min(0.16, (now-last_progress)*0.002)
          width=float(rng.choice([0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,1.0]))
          a=_perturb(a,rng,sig,width)
          fa,c=_convolve(a,L)
          last_progress=now
          T=min(T*1.5, 1.0)
          it=0
          continue
       if now - last_best > 120.0:
          sig=0.12 + min(0.30, (now-last_best)*0.002)
          width=float(rng.choice([0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]))
          a=_perturb(best_a,rng,sig,width)
          fa,c=_convolve(a,L)
          last_progress=now
          T=min(T*2.0, 1.0)
          it=0
          continue
       G=_log_gradient(a,c,L,fa,T)
       gscale=float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
       if gscale < 1e-14:
          if now-last_progress>5.0:
             a=_perturb(a,rng,0.05,0.1)
             fa,c=_convolve(a,L); last_progress=now; continue
          break
       improved=False
       e=eta
       for back in range(3):
          cand=_mul_update(a,G,e)
          fa2,c2=_convolve(cand,L)
          obj2=2.0*n*float(c2.max())
          if obj2 < obj - 1e-14:
             a=cand; obj=obj2; fa=fa2; c=c2; improved=True; break
          e *= 0.5
       if improved:
          last_progress=now
          T=max(0.015, T*0.999)
          if obj < best_obj - 1e-9:
             eta=min(eta*1.05, 1.2)
       else:
          if (it & 31) == 0:  # every 32? but it increments
              T=max(0.01, T*0.85)
              eta=max(0.02, eta*0.75)
       it += 1
       if it % 2000 ==0:
          print(f"[{tag}] log it={it} T={T:.4f} eta={eta:.3f} obj={obj:.6f} best={best_obj:.6f}")
    return best_a,best_obj
```
Operators: `it & 31` works int. Use modulo for clarity.

Potential issue: When no improvement, `it` increments. If backtracking fails many times, after 30s perturb. Good. If improvement step accepted but objective maybe not best due local, last_progress updated. Good.

But if `_mul_update` candidate is exactly a due G zero after centering? backtrack fails; perturb after 30s.

- `run_end` could be before now; while loop won't run. Good.

`_run_c` similar. Use T in r units, T_start maybe 0.5, T_min 0.02. Step eta maybe 0.1 (Euclidean project). Backtrack 3.
```
def _run_c(...):
  a=_norm1; fa,c; obj; best; T=T_init; eta=eta_init
  while ...:
    obj; update best; perturb logic similar (30s)
    if time-last_best>120: perturb best with larger
    s_w=_c_weights(c,T,n); H=_corr; g=2H; g-=mean
    if small -> perturb
    backtrack: cand=_proj_update(a,g,e); fa2,c2; obj2=2nmax; if improve accept
    if improved: T=max(0.02,T*0.999); eta adapt
    else: every 32 T*=0.85 eta*=0.75
```
Projection can set current exactly previous if step zero. Good.

In `_run_c`, T=temperature for r=c*n. If T small, weights sharp. If T large 0.5, broad.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if budget_s <= 20: budget_s=40
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 6.0)
    final_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 2.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
        raw=height_sequence_1
        p=np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim==1 and p.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p=np.maximum(p,0.0); s=float(p.sum())
            if 200 <= p.size <= 12000 and s>1e-6:
                parent=p/s
    except Exception:
        parent=None
    if parent is not None:
       n=int(parent.size)
    else:
       n=4096
    L=_fft_len(n)
    # initial best
    inits=[]
    if parent is not None: inits.append((parent, 'parent'))
    central=_central(n)
    inits.append((central,'central'))
    sym=_central_sym(n)
    inits.append((sym,'sym'))
    rand=_random_seq(n,rng,0)
    inits.append((rand,'rand'))
    best_a=None; best_obj=inf
    for a,tag in inits:
       ao=_objective(a,n,L)
       print(f"init {tag} obj={ao:.6f}")
       if ao<best_obj: best_obj=ao; best_a=a.copy()
    # phase 1: if parent, log parent else log central
    phase_budget = max(1.0, budget_s - 30.0)
    if parent is not None:
       end1 = t0 + 0.30*phase_budget
       bA,bO=_run_log(parent,n,L,T_init=0.12,eta_init=0.35,run_end=end1,tag='parent',rng=rng)
       if bO<best_obj: best_obj=bO; best_a=bA.copy()
    else:
       end1 = t0 + 0.35*phase_budget
       bA,bO=_run_log(central,n,L,T_init=0.25,eta_init=0.35,run_end=end1,tag='central1',rng=rng)
       update
    # phase2: central c phase
    end2 = t0 + 0.48*phase_budget
    aC,_=_run_c(central,n,L,T_init=0.5,eta_init=0.15,run_end=end2,tag='centralC',rng=rng)
    # maybe if central c better, update best
    oc=_objective(aC,n,L); if oc<best_obj: best=...
    # phase3: log from aC for slice
    end3 = t0 + 0.58*phase_budget
    if time.monotonic() < end3:
       bC,bCO=_run_log(aC,n,L,T_init=0.10,eta=0.30,run_end=end3,tag='centralL',rng=rng)
       update
    # phase4: sym/random c/log
    end4 = t0 + 0.68*phase_budget
    aS,_=_run_c(sym,n,L,T_init=0.5,eta=0.15,run_end=end4,tag='symC',rng=rng)
    os_=... update
    end5 = t0 + 0.78*phase_budget
    if time < end5:
       bS,bSO=_run_log(best_a? or aS? choose aS if better? use aS, T=0.1, run_end=end5,tag='symL')
       update
    # phase5 final aggressive on best
    bF,bFO=_run_log(best_a,n,L,T_init=0.05,eta_init=0.5,run_end=hard_deadline,tag='final',rng=rng)
    if bFO<best_obj: update
    # if still time, run c on best small?
    if time < final_deadline-5:
       bF2,_? maybe run_c with T=0.05 for remaining? Could improve exact.
    # final validate
    result=_norm1(best_a)
    # ensure length n, not bool, finite
    result=[float(x) for x in result]
    # maybe evaluate and if inf fallback central
    try:
       sc=float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
       sc=float('inf')
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
       result=[float(x) for x in central]
       try: sc=float(evaluate_sequence(result))
       except: pass
    print final
    return result
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a)^2).

Strategy
--------
Work with sequences normalized to sum 1, so the internal objective is
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)).

The optimizer uses several complementary local searches:

1. Log-domain multiplicative gradient descent for the scale-invariant
   objective max(log conv) - 2 log(sum).  Lag weights are obtained from
   a log-softmax over convolution coefficients.

2. Projected Euclidean gradient descent for a softened max-convolution
   objective, used as a restart/nester step.

3. Sparse/mixed initializers based on the central-binomial profile
   (1-x)^(-1/2), including oscillatory and right-tailed variants.

4. Adaptive temperature annealing, backtracking line search, periodic
   block perturbations, and occasional cross-method moves to escape
   local minima.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(x):
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _fft_len(n):
    return _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)


def _ones_norm(n):
    return np.full(int(n), 1.0 / int(n), dtype=np.float64)


def _norm1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _proj1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _convolve(a, L, fa=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if fa is None:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    spec = fa * fa.conj()
    c = np.fft.irfft(spec, n=L)
    m = 2 * a.size - 1
    c = c[:m]
    if c.size:
        if float(c.min()) < 0.0:
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return fa, c


def _corr_w_a(w, a, L, fa=None):
    """
    H[j] = sum_i a[i] * w[j+i], for j = 0..n-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size < L:
        wp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        wp[:w.size] = w
        w = wp
    else:
        w = w[:L]

    if fa is None:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, n=L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(W * fa.conj(), n=L)
    h = h[:n]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return h


def _objective(a, n, L, fa=None, c=None):
    if c is None:
        _, c = _convolve(a, L, fa)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mc = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def _log_weights(c, T):
    """
    Softmax weights over log convolution coefficients.
    """
    n = c.size
    c_safe = np.where(c > 1e-300, c, 0.0)
    mx = float(c_safe.max())
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    logc = np.log(c_safe)
    mxlog = float(logc.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mxlog):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        out[idx] = 1.0
        return out

    x = (logc - mxlog) / float(T)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, -745.0)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    ss = float(e.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        out[idx] = 1.0
        return out
    return e / ss


def _log_gradient(a, c, L, fa, T):
    """
    Gradient in log coordinates for max(log c) - 2 log(sum), with
    log-softmax smoothing on the max.
    """
    sw = _log_weights(c, T)
    w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = c > 1e-300
    w[pos] = sw[pos] / c[pos]

    H = _corr_w_a(w, a, L, fa)
    G = 2.0 * a * H - 2.0 * a
    G = np.where(np.isfinite(G), G, 0.0)
    G -= float(np.mean(G))
    return G


def _c_weights(c, T, n):
    """
    Softmax weights for c*n (so that T is roughly in peak-scale units).
    """
    r = c * float(n)
    mx = float(r.max())
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(r.size, 1.0 / r.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (r - mx) / float(T)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    ss = float(e.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        out = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        out[idx] = 1.0
        return out
    return e / ss


def _c_gradient(a, c, L, fa, T, n):
    """
    Projected-gradient direction for softened max convolution.
    """
    sw = _c_weights(c, T, n)
    H = _corr_w_a(sw, a, L, fa)
    g = 2.0 * H
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g -= float(np.mean(g))
    return g


def _mul_update(a, G, eta):
    gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
    if gscale < 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(gscale):
        return a.copy()
    expo = np.clip(-float(eta) * G / gscale, -40.0, 40.0)
    b = a * np.exp(expo)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    ss = float(b.sum())
    if ss < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        return a.copy()
    return b / ss


def _proj_update(a, g, eta):
    gs = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if gs < 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(gs):
        return a.copy()
    cand = a - float(eta) * g / gs
    return _proj1(cand)


def _central_raw(n):
    u = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return u
    u[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(n - 1):
        u[i + 1] = u[i] * (2.0 * i + 1.0) / (2.0 * i + 2.0)
    return u


def _central(n):
    u = _central_raw(n)
    return _norm1(u)


def _central_mix(n, alpha):
    u = _central_raw(n)
    v = u + float(alpha) * u[::-1]
    return _norm1(v)


def _central_osc(n, freq, eps):
    u = _central_raw(n)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    factor = 1.0 + float(eps) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * t / float(n))
    v = u * factor
    return _norm1(v)


def _random_seq(n, rng, kind=0):
    n = int(n)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if kind == 0:
        x = np.abs(rng.normal(1.0, 0.75, size=n))
        x *= np.exp(-0.25 * t / n)
    elif kind == 1:
        x = 1.0 + 0.45 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * rng.random() * t * 3.0 / n)
        x += 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * rng.random() * t * 7.0 / n)
    else:
        x = rng.uniform(0.25, 1.25, size=n)
        x *= np.exp(-0.10 * t / n)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.02)
    return _norm1(x)


def _perturb(a, rng, sigma, width=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if width is None or width >= 1.0:
        s = 0
        w = n
    else:
        w = max(2, int(n * float(width)))
        if w > n:
            w = n
        s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))

    b = a.copy()
    b[s:s + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=w))
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    ss = float(b.sum())
    if ss < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ss):
        return _ones_norm(n)
    return b / ss


def _item_first(item):
    return item[0]


def _run_log(a0, n, L, T_init, eta_init, run_end, tag, rng):
    a = _norm1(a0)
    fa, c = _convolve(a, L)
    obj = _objective(a, n, L, fa, c)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj

    T = float(T_init)
    eta = float(eta_init)
    eta_c = 0.12

    last_progress = time.monotonic()
    last_best = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    fail_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < run_end:
        now = time.monotonic()

        obj = _objective(a, n, L, fa, c)
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj
            best_a = a.copy()
            last_best = now

        # Mild exploration if stuck.
        if now - last_progress > 30.0:
            sig = 0.05 + min(0.18, 0.003 * (now - last_progress))
            width = float(rng.choice([0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 1.0]))
            a = _perturb(a, rng, sig, width)
            fa, c = _convolve(a, L)
            last_progress = now
            T = min(T * 1.5, 1.0)
            it = 0
            fail_count = 0
            continue

        # Stronger restart from the best point if long time without a new best.
        if now - last_best > 120.0:
            sig = 0.12 + min(0.35, 0.0025 * (now - last_best))
            width = float(rng.choice([0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 1.0]))
            a = _perturb(best_a, rng, sig, width)
            fa, c = _convolve(a, L)
            last_progress = now
            T = min(T * 2.0, 1.0)
            it = 0
            fail_count = 0
            continue

        G = _log_gradient(a, c, L, fa, T)
        gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
        if gscale < 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(gscale):
            if now - last_progress > 5.0:
                a = _perturb(a, rng, 0.05, 0.10)
                fa, c = _convolve(a, L)
                last_progress = now
                continue
            break

        improved = False
        e = eta
        for _ in range(3):
            cand = _mul_update(a, G, e)
            fa2, c2 = _convolve(cand, L)
            obj2 = _objective(cand, n, L, fa2, c2)
            if obj2 < obj - 1e-14:
                a = cand
                obj = obj2
                fa = fa2
                c = c2
                improved = True
                break
            e *= 0.5

        # If log-gradient keeps failing, try one projected Euclidean step.
        if not improved:
            fail_count += 1
            if (fail_count % 24) == 0:
                Tc = max(0.05, min(0.35, 0.18 + 0.5 * T))
                gc = _c_gradient(a, c, L, fa, Tc, n)
                gs = float(np.max(np.abs(gc)))
                if gs > 1e-14 and np.isfinite(gs):
                    ec = eta_c
                    for _ in range(2):
                        cand = _proj_update(a, gc, ec)
                        fa2, c2 = _convolve(cand, L)
                        obj2 = _objective(cand, n, L, fa2, c2)
                        if obj2 < obj - 1e-14:
                            a = cand
                            obj = obj2
                            fa = fa2
                            c = c2
                            improved = True
                            eta_c = ec
                            break
                        ec *= 0.5

        if improved:
            last_progress = now
            fail_count = 0
            T = max(0.005, T * 0.999)
            if obj < best_obj - 1e-9:
                eta = min(eta * 1.04, 1.2)
        else:
            if (fail_count % 32) == 0:
                T = max(0.005, T * 0.85)
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.75)

        it += 1
        if it % 5000 == 0:
            print(f"[{tag}] log it={it} T={T:.4f} eta={eta:.3f} obj={obj:.6f} best={best_obj:.6f}")

    return best_a, best_obj


def _run_c(a0, n, L, T_init, eta_init, run_end, tag, rng):
    a = _norm1(a0)
    fa, c = _convolve(a, L)
    obj = _objective(a, n, L, fa, c)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj

    T = float(T_init)
    eta = float(eta_init)

    last_progress = time.monotonic()
    last_best = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    fail_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < run_end:
        now = time.monotonic()

        obj = _objective(a, n, L, fa, c)
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj
            best_a = a.copy()
            last_best = now

        if now - last_progress > 35.0:
            sig = 0.05 + min(0.20, 0.003 * (now - last_progress))
            width = float(rng.choice([0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 1.0]))
            a = _perturb(a, rng, sig, width)
            fa, c = _convolve(a, L)
            last_progress = now
            T = min(T * 1.5, 1.0)
            it = 0
            fail_count = 0
            continue

        if now - last_best > 150.0:
            sig = 0.14 + min(0.40, 0.0025 * (now - last_best))
            width = float(rng.choice([0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 1.0]))
            a = _perturb(best_a, rng, sig, width)
            fa, c = _convolve(a, L)
            last_progress = now
            T = min(T * 2.0, 1.0)
            it = 0
            fail_count = 0
            continue

        g = _c_gradient(a, c, L, fa, T, n)
        gs = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gs < 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(gs):
            if now - last_progress > 5.0:
                a = _perturb(a, rng, 0.05, 0.10)
                fa, c = _convolve(a, L)
                last_progress = now
                continue
            break

        improved = False
        e = eta
        for _ in range(3):
            cand = _proj_update(a, g, e)
            fa2, c2 = _convolve(cand, L)
            obj2 = _objective(cand, n, L, fa2, c2)
            if obj2 < obj - 1e-14:
                a = cand
                obj = obj2
                fa = fa2
                c = c2
                improved = True
                break
            e *= 0.5

        if improved:
            last_progress = now
            fail_count = 0
            T = max(0.02, T * 0.999)
            if obj < best_obj - 1e-9:
                eta = min(eta * 1.04, 1.0)
        else:
            fail_count += 1
            if (fail_count % 32) == 0:
                T = max(0.02, T * 0.85)
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.75)

        it += 1
        if it % 5000 == 0:
            print(f"[{tag}] c it={it} T={T:.4f} eta={eta:.3f} obj={obj:.6f} best={best_obj:.6f}")

    return best_a, best_obj


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = max(20.0, float(budget_s))
    final_deadline = t0 + budget_s - 2.0
    hard_deadline = t0 + budget_s - 8.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if isinstance(seed, (int, np.integer)) else seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        p = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if 300 <= p.size <= 8000 and s > 1e-6:
                parent = p / s
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n = 4096

    L = _fft_len(n)

    raw_inits = []
    if parent is not None:
        raw_inits.append((parent, "parent"))

    raw_inits.append((_central(n), "central"))
    raw_inits.append((_central_mix(n, 0.12), "mix12"))
    raw_inits.append((_central_mix(n, 0.30), "mix30"))
    raw_inits.append((_central_mix(n, 0.80), "mix80"))
    raw_inits.append((_central_osc(n, 1, 0.10), "osc1"))
    raw_inits.append((_central_osc(n, 2, 0.12), "osc2"))
    raw_inits.append((_random_seq(n, rng, 0), "rand0"))

    init_metrics = []
    for a, tag in raw_inits:
        ao = _objective(a, n, L)
        if np.isfinite(ao):
            init_metrics.append((ao, a, tag))

    if not init_metrics:
        init_metrics.append((_objective(_central(n), n, L), _central(n), "fallback"))

    init_metrics.sort(key=_item_first)

    best_a = init_metrics[0][1].copy()
    best_obj = float(init_metrics[0][0])

    print(f"n={n} L={L} init_best={best_obj:.6f} ({init_metrics[0][2]})")
    for ao, _, tag in init_metrics[:8]:
        print(f"  init {tag}: {ao:.6f}")

    phase_budget = max(1.0, budget_s - 30.0)

    m = len(init_metrics)

    # Phase 1: best initial point, log-domain descent.
    end1 = t0 + (0.30 if parent is not None else 0.34) * phase_budget
    if time.monotonic() < end1:
        init0 = init_metrics[0]
        T0 = 0.11 if init0[0] < 1.55 else 0.28
        a1, o1 = _run_log(init0[1], n, L, T0, 0.35, end1, f"P1_{init0[2]}", rng)
        if o1 < best_obj:
            best_obj = o1
            best_a = a1.copy()
            print(f"After P1 {init0[2]}: obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    # Phase 2: second best initial point, projected convex-like descent.
    end2 = t0 + 0.48 * phase_budget
    if time.monotonic() < end2 and m > 1:
        init1 = init_metrics[1]
        a2, o2 = _run_c(init1[1], n, L, 0.45, 0.16, end2, f"P2_{init1[2]}", rng)
        if o2 < best_obj:
            best_obj = o2
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print(f"After P2 {init1[2]}: obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    # Phase 3: continue that second basin with log descent.
    end3 = t0 + 0.60 * phase_budget
    if time.monotonic() < end3 and m > 1:
        a3, o3 = _run_log(a2, n, L, 0.10, 0.30, end3, f"P3_{init1[2]}", rng)
        if o3 < best_obj:
            best_obj = o3
            best_a = a3.copy()
            print(f"After P3 {init1[2]}: obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    # Phase 4: third initial point or fallback central variant.
    end4 = t0 + 0.72 * phase_budget
    if time.monotonic() < end4:
        if m > 2:
            init2 = init_metrics[2]
        else:
            init2 = (_objective(_central_mix(n, 0.5), n, L), _central_mix(n, 0.5), "mix50")
        a4, o4 = _run_c(init2[1], n, L, 0.45, 0.16, end4, f"P4_{init2[2]}", rng)
        if o4 < best_obj:
            best_obj = o4
            best_a = a4.copy()
            print(f"After P4 {init2[2]}: obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    # Phase 5: log-descent on that fourth result.
    end5 = t0 + 0.80 * phase_budget
    if time.monotonic() < end5:
        a5, o5 = _run_log(a4, n, L, 0.09, 0.28, end5, f"P5_{init2[2]}", rng)
        if o5 < best_obj:
            best_obj = o5
            best_a = a5.copy()
            print(f"After P5 {init2[2]}: obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    # Final aggressive polish from the global best.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline:
        aF, oF = _run_log(best_a, n, L, 0.05, 0.45, hard_deadline, "final", rng)
        if oF < best_obj:
            best_obj = oF
            best_a = aF.copy()
        print(f"After final log: obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    # Last short projected polish if time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 4.0:
        aG, oG = _run_c(best_a, n, L, 0.08, 0.10, final_deadline - 1.0, "final_c", rng)
        if oG < best_obj:
            best_obj = oG
            best_a = aG.copy()
        print(f"After final_c: obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    final_a = _norm1(best_a)

    # Sanity: if anything non-finite slipped in, rebuild from central.
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(final_a)) or float(final_a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        final_a = _central(n)

    result = [float(x) for x in final_a]

    try:
        real_score = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
        real_score = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(real_score):
        # Very conservative fallback.
        final_a = _central(n)
        result = [float(x) for x in final_a]
        try:
            real_score = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
        except Exception:
            real_score = float("inf")

    print(f"FINAL: internal={best_obj:.6f} evaluated={real_score:.6f} n={n}")
    return result
```
